- **i18n-audit**: Internationalization completeness audit and key synchronization. Finds missing translation keys, hardcoded strings, pluralization bugs, RTL layout issues, date/number formatting gaps, and untranslated copy. Supports i18next, react-intl, vue-i18n, next-intl, and custom JSON locale files. Use when adding a new language, auditing translation completeness, or debugging locale mismatch errors. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/i18n-audit))

- **i18n-expert**: Complete internationalization/localization setup and auditing for UI codebases. Configure i18n frameworks, replace hard-coded strings with translation keys, ensure locale parity between en-US and zh-CN, and validate pluralization and formatting. Use when setting up i18n for React/Next.js/Vue apps, auditing existing implementations, replacing hard-coded strings, ensuring proper error code mapping, or validating pluralization and date/time/number formatting across locales ([daymade/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/daymade/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/i18n-expert))

Supports i18next, react-intl, vue-i18n, next-intl, and custom JSON locale files. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/i18n-sync))
- **i18n-tools**: Code-aware UI localization readiness analysis: inventory UI strings from source, draft a glossary, and flag the structural blockers (RTL, fonts, locale loading, hand-built formatting) a string catalog can't show. - **i18n-translate**: Copywriting-grade, context-aware localization for any managed app - a transcreation loop (draft -&gt; typed MQM estimate -&gt; gated refine) with an engineering guardrail. Portable across i18n stacks via a per-repo contract artifact; keeps a glossary, per-locale style guides, and gold exemplar pairs in the target repo so run N+1 stays consistent with run N. Invoke with /i18n-translate &lt;mode&gt; [locale] [s ([xkazm04/ai-registry](https://github.com/xkazm04/ai-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/i18n-translate))

- **intent**: Intent-based task resolution. When stuck, facing ambiguity, or about to ask a clarifying question, first recover the task's underlying intent from available context - the latest prompt, earlier prompts, project goals, plans, and prior corrections - then answer low-risk questions from that intent and make intent-aligned decisions. Escalate to the user only when the ambiguity is material and cannot be resolved from context; and when neither the intent nor a safe default can be inferred at all, an escape hatch stops and names the gap rather than fabricating an answer. ([florianbuetow/claude-code](https://github.com/florianbuetow/claude-code/tree/HEAD/plugins/intent))
